// You can also attach the data handler separately if you don't want to
// do so in the constructor.
gzipStream.ondata = (chunk, final) =&gt; { ... }

// Since this is synchronous, all errors will be thrown by stream.push()
gzipStream.push(chunk1);
gzipStream.push(chunk2);

...

// You should mark the last chunk by using true in the second argument
// In addition to being necessary for the stream to work properly, this
// will also set the isLast parameter in the handler to true.
gzipStream.push(lastChunk, true);

console.log(outStr); // The compressed binary string is now available

// The options parameter for compression streams is optional; you can
// provide one parameter (the handler) or none at all if you set
// deflateStream.ondata later.
const deflateStream = new fflate.Deflate((chunk, final) =&gt; {
  console.log(chunk, final);
});

// If you want to create a stream from strings, use EncodeUTF8
const utfEncode = new fflate.EncodeUTF8((data, final) =&gt; {
  // Chaining streams together is done by pushing to the
  // next stream in the handler for the previous stream
  deflateStream.push(data, final);
});

utfEncode.push('Hello'.repeat(1000));
utfEncode.push(' '.repeat(100));
utfEncode.push('world!'.repeat(10), true);

// The deflateStream has logged the compressed data

const inflateStream = new fflate.Inflate();
inflateStream.ondata = (decompressedChunk, final) =&gt; { ... };

let stringData = '';

// Streaming UTF-8 decode is available too
const utfDecode = new fflate.DecodeUTF8((data, final) =&gt; {
  stringData += data;
});

// Decompress streams auto-detect the compression method, as the
// non-streaming decompress() method does.
const dcmpStrm = new fflate.Decompress((chunk, final) =&gt; {
  console.log(chunk, 'was encoded with GZIP, Zlib, or DEFLATE');
  utfDecode.push(chunk, final);
});

dcmpStrm.push(zlibJSONData1);
dcmpStrm.push(zlibJSONData2, true);

// This succeeds; the UTF-8 decoder chained with the unknown compression format
// stream to reach a string as a sink.
console.log(JSON.parse(stringData));"><pre><span class="pl-c">// This example uses synchronous streams, but for the best experience</span>
<span class="pl-c">// you'll definitely want to use asynchronous streams.</span>

<span class="pl-k">let</span> <span class="pl-s1">outStr</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s">''</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">gzipStream</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-s1">fflate</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">Gzip</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-c1">level</span>: <span class="pl-c1">9</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">chunk</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">isLast</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// accumulate in an inefficient binary string (just an example)</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">outStr</span> <span class="pl-c1">+=</span> <span class="pl-s1">fflate</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">strFromU8</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">chunk</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">true</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-c">// You can also attach the data handler separately if you don't want to</span>
<span class="pl-c">// do so in the constructor.</span>
<span class="pl-s1">gzipStream</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">ondata</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">chunk</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">final</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span> ... <span class="pl-kos">}</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Since this is synchronous, all errors will be thrown by stream.push()</span>
<span class="pl-s1">gzipStream</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">push</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">chunk1</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">gzipStream</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">push</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">chunk2</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

...

<span class="pl-c">// You should mark the last chunk by using true in the second argument</span>
<span class="pl-c">// In addition to being necessary for the stream to work properly, this</span>
<span class="pl-c">// will also set the isLast parameter in the handler to true.</span>
<span class="pl-s1">gzipStream</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">push</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">lastChunk</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">true</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">log</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">outStr</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span> <span class="pl-c">// The compressed binary string is now available</span>

<span class="pl-c">// The options parameter for compression streams is optional; you can</span>
<span class="pl-c">// provide one parameter (the handler) or none at all if you set</span>
<span class="pl-c">// deflateStream.ondata later.</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">deflateStream</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-s1">fflate</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">Deflate</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">chunk</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">final</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">log</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">chunk</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">final</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-c">// If you want to create a stream from strings, use EncodeUTF8</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">utfEncode</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-s1">fflate</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">EncodeUTF8</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">data</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">final</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// Chaining streams together is done by pushing to the</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// next stream in the handler for the previous stream</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">deflateStream</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">push</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">data</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">final</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-s1">utfEncode</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">push</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'Hello'</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">repeat</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-c1">1000</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">utfEncode</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">push</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">' '</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">repeat</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-c1">100</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">utfEncode</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">push</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'world!'</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">repeat</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-c1">10</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">true</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-c">// The deflateStream has logged the compressed data</span>

<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">inflateStream</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-s1">fflate</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">Inflate</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">inflateStream</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">ondata</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">decompressedChunk</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">final</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span> ... <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-k">let</span> <span class="pl-s1">stringData</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s">''</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Streaming UTF-8 decode is available too</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">utfDecode</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-s1">fflate</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">DecodeUTF8</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">data</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">final</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">stringData</span> <span class="pl-c1">+=</span> <span class="pl-s1">data</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Decompress streams auto-detect the compression method, as the</span>
<span class="pl-c">// non-streaming decompress() method does.</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">dcmpStrm</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-s1">fflate</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">Decompress</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">chunk</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">final</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">log</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">chunk</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">'was encoded with GZIP, Zlib, or DEFLATE'</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">utfDecode</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">push</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">chunk</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">final</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-s1">dcmpStrm</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">push</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">zlibJSONData1</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">dcmpStrm</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">push</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">zlibJSONData2</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">true</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-c">// This succeeds; the UTF-8 decoder chained with the unknown compression format</span>
<span class="pl-c">// stream to reach a string as a sink.</span>
<span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">log</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-c1">JSON</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">parse</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">stringData</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">You can create multi-file ZIP archives easily as well. Note that by default, compression is enabled for all files, which is not useful when ZIPping many PNGs, JPEGs, PDFs, etc. because those formats are already compressed. You should either override the level on a per-file basis or globally to avoid wasting resources.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// Note that the asynchronous version (see below) runs in parallel and
// is *much* (up to 3x) faster for larger archives.
const zipped = fflate.zipSync({
  // Directories can be nested structures, as in an actual filesystem
  'dir1': {
    'nested': {
      // You can use Unicode in filenames
      '你好.txt': fflate.strToU8('Hey there!')
    },
    // You can also manually write out a directory path
    'other/tmp.txt': new Uint8Array([97, 98, 99, 100])
  },

  // You can also provide compression options
  'massiveImage.bmp': [aMassiveFile, {
    level: 9,
    mem: 12
  }],
  // PNG is pre-compressed; no need to waste time
  'superTinyFile.png': [aPNGFile, { level: 0 }],

  // Directories take options too
  'exec': [{
    'hello.sh': [fflate.strToU8('echo hello world'), {
      // ZIP only: Set the operating system to Unix
      os: 3,
      // ZIP only: Make this file executable on Unix
      attrs: 0o755 &lt;&lt; 16
    }]
  }, {
    // ZIP and GZIP support mtime (defaults to current time)
    mtime: new Date('10/20/2020')
  }]
}, {
  // These options are the defaults for all files, but file-specific
  // options take precedence.
  level: 1,
  // Obfuscate last modified time by default 
  mtime: new Date('1/1/1980')
});

// If you write the zipped data to myzip.zip and unzip, the folder
// structure will be outputted as:

// myzip.zip (original file)
// dir1
// |-&gt; nested
// |   |-&gt; 你好.txt
// |-&gt; other
// |   |-&gt; tmp.txt
// massiveImage.bmp
// superTinyFile.png

// When decompressing, folders are not nested; all filepaths are fully
// written out in the keys. For example, the return value may be:
// { 'nested/directory/structure.txt': Uint8Array(2) [97, 97] }
const decompressed = fflate.unzipSync(zipped, {
  // You may optionally supply a filter for files. By default, all files in a
  // ZIP archive are extracted, but a filter can save resources by telling
  // the library not to decompress certain files
  filter(file) {
    // Don't decompress the massive image or any files larger than 10 MiB
    return file.name != 'massiveImage.bmp' &amp;&amp; file.originalSize &lt;= 10_000_000;
  }
});"><pre><span class="pl-c">// Note that the asynchronous version (see below) runs in parallel and</span>
<span class="pl-c">// is *much* (up to 3x) faster for larger archives.</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">zipped</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">fflate</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">zipSync</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// Directories can be nested structures, as in an actual filesystem</span>
  <span class="pl-s">'dir1'</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-s">'nested'</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
      <span class="pl-c">// You can use Unicode in filenames</span>
      <span class="pl-s">'你好.txt'</span>: <span class="pl-s1">fflate</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">strToU8</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'Hey there!'</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
    <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
    <span class="pl-c">// You can also manually write out a directory path</span>
    <span class="pl-s">'other/tmp.txt'</span>: <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-v">Uint8Array</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-c1">97</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">98</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">99</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">100</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>

  <span class="pl-c">// You can also provide compression options</span>
  <span class="pl-s">'massiveImage.bmp'</span>: <span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-s1">aMassiveFile</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">level</span>: <span class="pl-c1">9</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">mem</span>: <span class="pl-c1">12</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// PNG is pre-compressed; no need to waste time</span>
  <span class="pl-s">'superTinyFile.png'</span>: <span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-s1">aPNGFile</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-c1">level</span>: <span class="pl-c1">0</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>

  <span class="pl-c">// Directories take options too</span>
  <span class="pl-s">'exec'</span>: <span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-s">'hello.sh'</span>: <span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-s1">fflate</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">strToU8</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'echo hello world'</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
      <span class="pl-c">// ZIP only: Set the operating system to Unix</span>
      <span class="pl-c1">os</span>: <span class="pl-c1">3</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
      <span class="pl-c">// ZIP only: Make this file executable on Unix</span>
      <span class="pl-c1">attrs</span>: <span class="pl-c1">0o755</span> <span class="pl-c1">&lt;&lt;</span> <span class="pl-c1">16</span>
    <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-c">// ZIP and GZIP support mtime (defaults to current time)</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">mtime</span>: <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-v">Date</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'10/20/2020'</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// These options are the defaults for all files, but file-specific</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// options take precedence.</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">level</span>: <span class="pl-c1">1</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// Obfuscate last modified time by default </span>
  <span class="pl-c1">mtime</span>: <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-v">Date</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'1/1/1980'</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-c">// If you write the zipped data to myzip.zip and unzip, the folder</span>
<span class="pl-c">// structure will be outputted as:</span>

<span class="pl-c">// myzip.zip (original file)</span>
<span class="pl-c">// dir1</span>
<span class="pl-c">// |-&gt; nested</span>
<span class="pl-c">// |   |-&gt; 你好.txt</span>
<span class="pl-c">// |-&gt; other</span>
<span class="pl-c">// |   |-&gt; tmp.txt</span>
<span class="pl-c">// massiveImage.bmp</span>
<span class="pl-c">// superTinyFile.png</span>

<span class="pl-c">// When decompressing, folders are not nested; all filepaths are fully</span>
<span class="pl-c">// written out in the keys. For example, the return value may be:</span>
<span class="pl-c">// { 'nested/directory/structure.txt': Uint8Array(2) [97, 97] }</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">decompressed</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">fflate</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">unzipSync</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">zipped</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// You may optionally supply a filter for files. By default, all files in a</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// ZIP archive are extracted, but a filter can save resources by telling</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// the library not to decompress certain files</span>
  <span class="pl-en">filter</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">file</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-c">// Don't decompress the massive image or any files larger than 10 MiB</span>
    <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-s1">file</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">name</span> <span class="pl-c1">!=</span> <span class="pl-s">'massiveImage.bmp'</span> <span class="pl-c1">&amp;&amp;</span> <span class="pl-s1">file</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">originalSize</span> <span class="pl-c1">&lt;=</span> <span class="pl-c1">10_000_000</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">If you need extremely high performance or custom ZIP compression formats, you can use the highly-extensible ZIP streams. They take streams as both input and output. You can even use custom compression/decompression algorithms from other libraries, as long as they <a href="https://pkware.cachefly.net/webdocs/casestudies/APPNOTE.TXT" rel="nofollow">are defined in the ZIP spec</a> (see section 4.4.5). If you'd like more info on using custom compressors, <a href="https://github.com/101arrowz/fflate/discussions">feel free to ask</a>.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// ZIP object
// Can also specify zip.ondata outside of the constructor
const zip = new fflate.Zip((err, dat, final) =&gt; {
  if (!err) {
    // output of the streams
    console.log(dat, final);
  }
});

const helloTxt = new fflate.ZipDeflate('hello.txt', {
  level: 9
});

// Always add streams to ZIP archives before pushing to those streams
zip.add(helloTxt);

helloTxt.push(chunk1);
// Last chunk
helloTxt.push(chunk2, true);

// ZipPassThrough is like ZipDeflate with level 0, but allows for tree shaking
const nonStreamingFile = new fflate.ZipPassThrough('test.png');
zip.add(nonStreamingFile);
// If you have data already loaded, just .push(data, true)
nonStreamingFile.push(pngData, true);

// You need to call .end() after finishing
// This ensures the ZIP is valid
zip.end();

// Unzip object
const unzipper = new fflate.Unzip();

// This function will almost always have to be called. It is used to support
// compression algorithms such as BZIP2 or LZMA in ZIP files if just DEFLATE
// is not enough (though it almost always is).
// If your ZIP files are not compressed, this line is not needed.
unzipper.register(fflate.UnzipInflate);

const neededFiles = ['file1.txt', 'example.json'];

// Can specify handler in constructor too
unzipper.onfile = file =&gt; {
  // file.name is a string, file is a stream
  if (neededFiles.includes(file.name)) {
    file.ondata = (err, dat, final) =&gt; {
      // Stream output here
      console.log(dat, final);
    };
    
    console.log('Reading:', file.name);

    // File sizes are sometimes not set if the ZIP file did not encode
    // them, so you may want to check that file.size != undefined
    console.log('Compressed size', file.size);
    console.log('Decompressed size', file.originalSize);

    // You should only start the stream if you plan to use it to improve
    // performance. Only after starting the stream will ondata be called.
    // This method will throw if the compression method hasn't been registered
    file.start();
  }
};

// Try to keep under 5,000 files per chunk to avoid stack limit errors
// For example, if all files are a few kB, multi-megabyte chunks are OK
// If files are mostly under 100 bytes, 64kB chunks are the limit
unzipper.push(zipChunk1);
unzipper.push(zipChunk2);
unzipper.push(zipChunk3, true);"><pre><span class="pl-c">// ZIP object</span>
<span class="pl-c">// Can also specify zip.ondata outside of the constructor</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">zip</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-s1">fflate</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">Zip</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">err</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">dat</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">final</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-c1">!</span><span class="pl-s1">err</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-c">// output of the streams</span>
    <span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">log</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">dat</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">final</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">helloTxt</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-s1">fflate</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">ZipDeflate</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'hello.txt'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">level</span>: <span class="pl-c1">9</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Always add streams to ZIP archives before pushing to those streams</span>
<span class="pl-s1">zip</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">add</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">helloTxt</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-s1">helloTxt</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">push</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">chunk1</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-c">// Last chunk</span>
<span class="pl-s1">helloTxt</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">push</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">chunk2</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">true</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-c">// ZipPassThrough is like ZipDeflate with level 0, but allows for tree shaking</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">nonStreamingFile</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-s1">fflate</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">ZipPassThrough</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'test.png'</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">zip</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">add</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">nonStreamingFile</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-c">// If you have data already loaded, just .push(data, true)</span>
<span class="pl-s1">nonStreamingFile</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">push</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">pngData</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">true</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-c">// You need to call .end() after finishing</span>
<span class="pl-c">// This ensures the ZIP is valid</span>
<span class="pl-s1">zip</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">end</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Unzip object</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">unzipper</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-s1">fflate</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">Unzip</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-c">// This function will almost always have to be called. It is used to support</span>
<span class="pl-c">// compression algorithms such as BZIP2 or LZMA in ZIP files if just DEFLATE</span>
<span class="pl-c">// is not enough (though it almost always is).</span>
<span class="pl-c">// If your ZIP files are not compressed, this line is not needed.</span>
<span class="pl-s1">unzipper</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">register</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">fflate</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">UnzipInflate</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">neededFiles</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-s">'file1.txt'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">'example.json'</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Can specify handler in constructor too</span>
<span class="pl-s1">unzipper</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">onfile</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">file</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// file.name is a string, file is a stream</span>
  <span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">neededFiles</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">includes</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">file</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">name</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-s1">file</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">ondata</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">err</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">dat</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">final</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
      <span class="pl-c">// Stream output here</span>
      <span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">log</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">dat</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">final</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
    <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
    
    <span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">log</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'Reading:'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">file</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">name</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

    <span class="pl-c">// File sizes are sometimes not set if the ZIP file did not encode</span>
    <span class="pl-c">// them, so you may want to check that file.size != undefined</span>
    <span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">log</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'Compressed size'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">file</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">size</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
    <span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">log</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'Decompressed size'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">file</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">originalSize</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

    <span class="pl-c">// You should only start the stream if you plan to use it to improve</span>
    <span class="pl-c">// performance. Only after starting the stream will ondata be called.</span>
    <span class="pl-c">// This method will throw if the compression method hasn't been registered</span>
    <span class="pl-s1">file</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">start</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Try to keep under 5,000 files per chunk to avoid stack limit errors</span>
<span class="pl-c">// For example, if all files are a few kB, multi-megabyte chunks are OK</span>
<span class="pl-c">// If files are mostly under 100 bytes, 64kB chunks are the limit</span>
<span class="pl-s1">unzipper</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">push</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">zipChunk1</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">unzipper</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">push</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">zipChunk2</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">unzipper</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">push</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">zipChunk3</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">true</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">As you may have guessed, there is an asynchronous version of every method as well. Unlike most libraries, this will cause the compression or decompression run in a separate thread entirely and automatically by using Web (or Node) Workers. This means that the processing will not block the main thread at all.</p>
<p dir="auto">Note that there is a significant initial overhead to using workers of about 50ms for each asynchronous function. For instance, if you call <code>unzip</code> ten times, the overhead only applies for the first call, but if you call <code>unzip</code> and <code>zlib</code>, they will each cause the 50ms delay. For small (under about 50kB) payloads, the asynchronous APIs will be much slower. However, if you're compressing larger files/multiple files at once, or if the synchronous API causes the main thread to hang for too long, the callback APIs are an order of magnitude better.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import {
  gzip, zlib, AsyncGzip, zip, unzip, strFromU8,
  Zip, AsyncZipDeflate, Unzip, AsyncUnzipInflate
} from 'fflate';

// Workers will work in almost any browser (even IE11!)
// All of the async APIs use a node-style callback as so:
const terminate = gzip(aMassiveFile, (err, data) =&gt; {
  if (err) {
    // The compressed data was likely corrupt, so we have to handle
    // the error.
    return;
  }
  // Use data however you like
  console.log(data.length);
});

if (needToCancel) {
  // The return value of any of the asynchronous APIs is a function that,
  // when called, will immediately cancel the operation. The callback
  // will not be called.
  terminate();
}

// If you wish to provide options, use the second argument.

// The consume option will render the data inside aMassiveFile unusable,
// but can improve performance and dramatically reduce memory usage.
zlib(aMassiveFile, { consume: true, level: 9 }, (err, data) =&gt; {
  // Use the data
});

// Asynchronous streams are similar to synchronous streams, but the
// handler has the error that occurred (if any) as the first parameter,
// and they don't block the main thread.

// Additionally, any buffers that are pushed in will be consumed and
// rendered unusable; if you need to use a buffer you push in, you
// should clone it first.
const gzs = new AsyncGzip({ level: 9, mem: 12, filename: 'hello.txt' });
let wasCallbackCalled = false;
gzs.ondata = (err, chunk, final) =&gt; {
  // Note the new err parameter
  if (err) {
    // Note that after this occurs, the stream becomes corrupt and must
    // be discarded. You can't continue pushing chunks and expect it to
    // work.
    console.error(err);
    return;
  }
  wasCallbackCalled = true;
}
gzs.push(chunk);

// Since the stream is asynchronous, the callback will not be called
// immediately. If such behavior is absolutely necessary (it shouldn't
// be), use synchronous streams.
console.log(wasCallbackCalled) // false

// To terminate an asynchronous stream's internal worker, call
// stream.terminate().
gzs.terminate();

// This is way faster than zipSync because the compression of multiple
// files runs in parallel. In fact, the fact that it's parallelized
// makes it faster than most standalone ZIP CLIs. The effect is most
// significant for multiple large files; less so for many small ones.
zip({ f1: aMassiveFile, 'f2.txt': anotherMassiveFile }, {
  // The options object is still optional, you can still do just
  // zip(archive, callback)
  level: 6
}, (err, data) =&gt; {
  // Save the ZIP file
});

// unzip is the only async function without support for consume option
// It is parallelized, so unzip is also often much faster than unzipSync
unzip(aMassiveZIPFile, (err, unzipped) =&gt; {
  // If the archive has data.xml, log it here
  console.log(unzipped['data.xml']);
  // Conversion to string
  console.log(strFromU8(unzipped['data.xml']))
});

// Streaming ZIP archives can accept asynchronous streams. This automatically
// uses multicore compression.
const zip = new Zip();
zip.ondata = (err, chunk, final) =&gt; { ... };
// The JSON and BMP are compressed in parallel
const exampleFile = new AsyncZipDeflate('example.json');
zip.add(exampleFile);
exampleFile.push(JSON.stringify({ large: 'object' }), true);
const exampleFile2 = new AsyncZipDeflate('example2.bmp', { level: 9 });
zip.add(exampleFile2);
exampleFile2.push(ec2a);
exampleFile2.push(ec2b);
exampleFile2.push(ec2c);
...
exampleFile2.push(ec2Final, true);
zip.end();

// Streaming Unzip should register the asynchronous inflation algorithm
// for parallel processing.
const unzip = new Unzip(stream =&gt; {
  if (stream.name.endsWith('.json')) {
    stream.ondata = (err, chunk, final) =&gt; { ... };
    stream.start();

    if (needToCancel) {
      // To cancel these streams, call .terminate()
      stream.terminate();
    }
  }
});
unzip.register(AsyncUnzipInflate);
unzip.push(data, true);"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">gzip</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">zlib</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-v">AsyncGzip</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">zip</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">unzip</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">strFromU8</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-v">Zip</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-v">AsyncZipDeflate</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-v">Unzip</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-v">AsyncUnzipInflate</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span> <span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s">'fflate'</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Workers will work in almost any browser (even IE11!)</span>
<span class="pl-c">// All of the async APIs use a node-style callback as so:</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">terminate</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">gzip</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">aMassiveFile</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">err</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">data</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">err</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-c">// The compressed data was likely corrupt, so we have to handle</span>
    <span class="pl-c">// the error.</span>
    <span class="pl-k">return</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// Use data however you like</span>
  <span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">log</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">data</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">length</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">needToCancel</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// The return value of any of the asynchronous APIs is a function that,</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// when called, will immediately cancel the operation. The callback</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// will not be called.</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">terminate</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span>

<span class="pl-c">// If you wish to provide options, use the second argument.</span>

<span class="pl-c">// The consume option will render the data inside aMassiveFile unusable,</span>
<span class="pl-c">// but can improve performance and dramatically reduce memory usage.</span>
<span class="pl-en">zlib</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">aMassiveFile</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-c1">consume</span>: <span class="pl-c1">true</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">level</span>: <span class="pl-c1">9</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">err</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">data</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// Use the data</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Asynchronous streams are similar to synchronous streams, but the</span>
<span class="pl-c">// handler has the error that occurred (if any) as the first parameter,</span>
<span class="pl-c">// and they don't block the main thread.</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Additionally, any buffers that are pushed in will be consumed and</span>
<span class="pl-c">// rendered unusable; if you need to use a buffer you push in, you</span>
<span class="pl-c">// should clone it first.</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">gzs</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-v">AsyncGzip</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-c1">level</span>: <span class="pl-c1">9</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">mem</span>: <span class="pl-c1">12</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">filename</span>: <span class="pl-s">'hello.txt'</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-k">let</span> <span class="pl-s1">wasCallbackCalled</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">false</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">gzs</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">ondata</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">err</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">chunk</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">final</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// Note the new err parameter</span>
  <span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">err</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-c">// Note that after this occurs, the stream becomes corrupt and must</span>
    <span class="pl-c">// be discarded. You can't continue pushing chunks and expect it to</span>
    <span class="pl-c">// work.</span>
    <span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">error</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">err</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
    <span class="pl-k">return</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">wasCallbackCalled</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">true</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span>
<span class="pl-s1">gzs</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">push</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">chunk</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Since the stream is asynchronous, the callback will not be called</span>
<span class="pl-c">// immediately. If such behavior is absolutely necessary (it shouldn't</span>
<span class="pl-c">// be), use synchronous streams.</span>
<span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">log</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">wasCallbackCalled</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c">// false</span>

<span class="pl-c">// To terminate an asynchronous stream's internal worker, call</span>
<span class="pl-c">// stream.terminate().</span>
<span class="pl-s1">gzs</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">terminate</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-c">// This is way faster than zipSync because the compression of multiple</span>
<span class="pl-c">// files runs in parallel. In fact, the fact that it's parallelized</span>
<span class="pl-c">// makes it faster than most standalone ZIP CLIs. The effect is most</span>
<span class="pl-c">// significant for multiple large files; less so for many small ones.</span>
<span class="pl-en">zip</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-c1">f1</span>: <span class="pl-s1">aMassiveFile</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">'f2.txt'</span>: <span class="pl-s1">anotherMassiveFile</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// The options object is still optional, you can still do just</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// zip(archive, callback)</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">level</span>: <span class="pl-c1">6</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">err</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">data</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// Save the ZIP file</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-c">// unzip is the only async function without support for consume option</span>
<span class="pl-c">// It is parallelized, so unzip is also often much faster than unzipSync</span>
<span class="pl-en">unzip</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">aMassiveZIPFile</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">err</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">unzipped</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// If the archive has data.xml, log it here</span>
  <span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">log</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">unzipped</span><span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-s">'data.xml'</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// Conversion to string</span>
  <span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">log</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-en">strFromU8</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">unzipped</span><span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-s">'data.xml'</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Streaming ZIP archives can accept asynchronous streams. This automatically</span>
<span class="pl-c">// uses multicore compression.</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">zip</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-v">Zip</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">zip</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">ondata</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">err</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">chunk</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">final</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span> ... <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-c">// The JSON and BMP are compressed in parallel</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">exampleFile</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-v">AsyncZipDeflate</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'example.json'</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">zip</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">add</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">exampleFile</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">exampleFile</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">push</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-c1">JSON</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">stringify</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-c1">large</span>: <span class="pl-s">'object'</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">true</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">exampleFile2</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-v">AsyncZipDeflate</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'example2.bmp'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-c1">level</span>: <span class="pl-c1">9</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">zip</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">add</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">exampleFile2</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">exampleFile2</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">push</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">ec2a</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">exampleFile2</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">push</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">ec2b</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">exampleFile2</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">push</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">ec2c</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
...
<span class="pl-s1">exampleFile2</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">push</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">ec2Final</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">true</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">zip</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">end</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Streaming Unzip should register the asynchronous inflation algorithm</span>
<span class="pl-c">// for parallel processing.</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">unzip</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-v">Unzip</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">stream</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">stream</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">name</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">endsWith</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'.json'</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-s1">stream</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">ondata</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">err</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">chunk</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">final</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span> ... <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
    <span class="pl-s1">stream</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">start</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

    <span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">needToCancel</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
      <span class="pl-c">// To cancel these streams, call .terminate()</span>
      <span class="pl-s1">stream</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">terminate</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
    <span class="pl-kos">}</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">unzip</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">register</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-v">AsyncUnzipInflate</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">unzip</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">push</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">data</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">true</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">See the <a href="https://github.com/101arrowz/fflate/blob/master/docs/README.md">documentation</a> for more detailed information about the API.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Bundle size estimates</h2><a id="user-content-bundle-size-estimates" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Bundle size estimates" href="#bundle-size-estimates"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The bundle size measurements for <code>fflate</code> on sites like Bundlephobia include every feature of the library and should be seen as an upper bound. As long as you are using tree shaking or dead code elimination, this table should give you a general idea of <code>fflate</code>'s bundle size for the features you need.</p>
<p dir="auto">The maximum bundle size that is possible with <code>fflate</code> is about 33kB (12.5kB gzipped) if you use every single feature, but feature parity with <code>pako</code> is only around 10kB (as opposed to 45kB from <code>pako</code>). If your bundle size increases dramatically after adding <code>fflate</code>, please <a href="https://github.com/101arrowz/fflate/issues/new">create an issue</a>.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">What makes <code>fflate</code> so fast?</h2><a id="user-content-what-makes-fflate-so-fast" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: What makes fflate so fast?" href="#what-makes-fflate-so-fast"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Many JavaScript compression/decompression libraries exist. However, the most popular one, <a href="https://npmjs.com/package/pako" rel="nofollow"><code>pako</code></a>, is merely a clone of Zlib rewritten nearly line-for-line in JavaScript. Although it is by no means poorly made, <code>pako</code> doesn't recognize the many differences between JavaScript and C, and therefore is suboptimal for performance. Moreover, even when minified, the library is 45 kB; it may not seem like much, but for anyone concerned with optimizing bundle size (especially library authors), it's more weight than necessary.</p>
<p dir="auto">Note that there exist some small libraries like <a href="https://npmjs.com/package/tiny-inflate" rel="nofollow"><code>tiny-inflate</code></a> for solely decompression, and with a minified size of 3 kB, it can be appealing; however, its performance is lackluster, typically 40% worse than <code>pako</code> in my tests.</p>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://github.com/photopea/UZIP.js"><code>UZIP.js</code></a> is both faster (by up to 25%) and smaller (14 kB minified) than <code>pako</code>, and it contains a variety of innovations that make it excellent for both performance and compression ratio. However, the developer made a variety of tiny mistakes and inefficient design choices that make it imperfect. Moreover, it does not support GZIP or Zlib data directly; one must remove the headers manually to use <code>UZIP.js</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto">So what makes <code>fflate</code> different? It takes the brilliant innovations of <code>UZIP.js</code> and optimizes them while adding direct support for GZIP and Zlib data. And unlike all of the above libraries, it uses ES Modules to allow for partial builds through tree shaking, meaning that it can rival even <code>tiny-inflate</code> in size while maintaining excellent performance. The end result is a library that, in total, weighs 8kB minified for the core build (3kB for decompression only and 5kB for compression only), is about 25% faster than <code>UZIP.js</code> or up to 50% faster than <code>pako</code>, and achieves the same or better compression ratio than the rest.</p>
<p dir="auto">Before you decide that <code>fflate</code> is the end-all compression library, you should note that JavaScript simply cannot rival the performance of a native program. If you're only using Node.js, it's probably better to use the <a href="https://nodejs.org/api/zlib.html" rel="nofollow">native Zlib bindings</a>, which tend to offer the best performance. Though note that even against Zlib, <code>fflate</code> is only around 30% slower in decompression and 10% slower in compression, and can still achieve better compression ratios!</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">What about <code>CompressionStream</code>?</h2><a id="user-content-what-about-compressionstream" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: What about CompressionStream?" href="#what-about-compressionstream"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Like <code>fflate</code>, the <a href="https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/Compression_Streams_API" rel="nofollow">Compression Streams API</a> provides DEFLATE, GZIP, and Zlib compression and decompression support. It's a good option if you'd like to compress or decompress data without installing any third-party libraries, and it wraps native Zlib bindings to achieve better performance than what most JavaScript programs can achieve.</p>
<p dir="auto">However, browsers do not offer any native non-streaming compression API, and <code>CompressionStream</code> has surprisingly poor performance on data already loaded into memory; <code>fflate</code> tends to be faster even for files that are dozens of megabytes large. Similarly, <code>fflate</code> is much faster for files under a megabyte because it avoids marshalling overheads. Even when streaming hundreds of megabytes of data, the native API usually performs between 30% faster and 10% slower than <code>fflate</code>. And Compression Streams have many other disadvantages - no ability to control compression level, poor support for older browsers, no ZIP support, etc.</p>
<p dir="auto">If you'd still prefer to depend upon a native browser API but want to support older browsers, you can use an <code>fflate</code>-based <a href="https://github.com/101arrowz/compression-streams-polyfill">Compression Streams ponyfill</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Browser support</h2><a id="user-content-browser-support" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Browser support" href="#browser-support"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>fflate</code> makes heavy use of typed arrays (<code>Uint8Array</code>, <code>Uint16Array</code>, etc.). Typed arrays can be polyfilled at the cost of performance, but the most recent browser that doesn't support them <a href="https://caniuse.com/typedarrays" rel="nofollow">is from 2011</a>, so I wouldn't bother.</p>
<p dir="auto">The asynchronous APIs also use <code>Worker</code>, which is not supported in a few browsers (however, the vast majority of browsers that support typed arrays support <code>Worker</code>).</p>
<p dir="auto">Other than that, <code>fflate</code> is completely ES3, meaning you probably won't even need a bundler to use it.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Testing</h2><a id="user-content-testing" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Testing" href="#testing"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">You can validate the performance of <code>fflate</code> with <code>npm test</code>. It validates that the module is working as expected, ensures the outputs are no more than 5% larger than competitors at max compression, and outputs performance metrics to <code>test/results</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Note that the time it takes for the CLI to show the completion of each test is not representative of the time each package took, so please check the JSON output if you want accurate measurements.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">License</h2><a id="user-content-license" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: License" href="#license"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

      data-analytics-event="{&quot;location&quot;:&quot;footer&quot;,&quot;action&quot;:&quot;cookies&quot;,&quot;context&quot;:&quot;subfooter&quot;,&quot;tag&quot;:&quot;link&quot;,&quot;label&quot;:&quot;cookies_link_subfooter_footer&quot;}"
    >
      Manage cookies
    </button>
  </cookie-consent-link>
</li>

  <li class="mx-2">
    <cookie-consent-link>
      <button
        type="button"
        class="Link--secondary underline-on-hover border-0 p-0 color-bg-transparent text-left"
        data-action="click:cookie-consent-link#showConsentManagement"
        data-analytics-event="{&quot;location&quot;:&quot;footer&quot;,&quot;action&quot;:&quot;dont_share_info&quot;,&quot;context&quot;:&quot;subfooter&quot;,&quot;tag&quot;:&quot;link&quot;,&quot;label&quot;:&quot;dont_share_info_link_subfooter_footer&quot;}"
      >
        Do not share my personal information
      </button>
    </cookie-consent-link>
  </li>

      </ul>
    </nav>
  </div>
</footer>

      data-cookie-consent-required="false"
